### 페이지 1

페이지 1 / 524 
 
how I think in English Stop translating in your head Start thinking in English.mp 
Hi, I am Ariel and today I am going to talk about my journey to think in English. 
 I grew up in a home where two languages were spoken. 
 At home we spoke a language that was not English. 
 I learned English at school. 
 I had to change my mind every time I spoke. 
 I had to think in one language and then change to another. 
 This made my words slow. 
 I felt lost when I spoke in class. 
 I often sat in silence as my friends spoke quickly. 
 I knew I had a big challenge. 
 I felt both sad and frustrated. 
 I did not want to feel that way anymore. 
 I remember a day in class. 
 My teacher asked a simple question. 
 My friends answered fast. 
 I sat there and thought, hard. 
 I had the answer in my mind, but my brain worked in two languages. 
 I had to translate each word. 
 I felt my heart race. 
 I felt my face warm with fear. 
 I wished I could speak quickly and simply. 
 I felt that my dream of thinking in English was far away. 
 That day, I promised myself that I must change.

### 페이지 2

페이지 2 / 524 
 
 I promised to set my mind free. 
 I promised to learn to speak without fear. 
 I began by changing small things in my life. 
 I changed my phone settings to English. 
 I set my computer to English, too. 
 I watched my favorite shows in English without subtitles. 
 I listened to English songs every day. 
 I even read small stories in English. 
 I filled my life with English words. 
 At first, this change felt strange. 
 My brain did not want to work in a new way. 
 I felt lost and unsure. 
 But I kept trying. 
 I spoke to myself in English. 
 When I woke up, I said, 
 I wake. 
 I brush my teeth. 
 I eat my breakfast. 
 I repeated these words in my mind. 
 I practiced them every day. 
 Slowly, my brain began to change. 
 I felt a small spark of hope. 
 I felt that I could learn to think in English. 
 Every day I took one small step.

 Step one, listen and imitate. 
 The power of listening. 
 One of the most effective ways to improve your pronunciation is to listen to how native 
speakers pronounce words and sentences. 
 You must actively listen to their intonation, rhythm, and pronunciation patterns. 
 Listening is the foundation of speaking because it trains your brain to recognize and 
reproduce sounds correctly. 
 Here's what you need to do. 
 Choose a source. 
 Pick a podcast, YouTube video, or TV show with native English speakers. 
 Make sure it's content you enjoy and can easily understand. 
 Listen actively. 
 Focus on how the speaker pronounces each word and Pay attention to the rise and fall 
of their voice, the speed at which they speak, and how they stress different words. 
 This will help you understand the rhythm of English. 
 Imitate and repeat. 
 After listening to a sentence, pause and repeat it out loud.

 By consistently listening and imitating, you will train your mouth and brain to produce 
English sounds more naturally. 
 Listening and imitating is one of the best tools you have. 
 Step two, speak slowly and clearly. 
 Many learners of English try to speak quickly because they think it will make them

 However, this can actually cause your pronunciation to become unclear.

understand you. 
 The key to improving your pronunciation is to speak slowly and clearly. 
 Don't rush. 
 Focus on each word and its sound. 
 Speaking slowly helps you pronounce words correctly and also gives you time to think 
about how to form the sounds. 
 Try this. 
 Slow down when you speak, especially when practicing. 
 Focus on pronouncing each sound clearly. 
 Break down difficult words into smaller parts.

 Step three, focus on problem sounds. 
 English has many sounds that are difficult to pronounce for non native speakers. 
 These include sounds that don't exist in your native language or sounds that change

 Practice tip: Stick your tongue slightly out between your teeth and blow air gently.

 The R sound in English is pronounced differently than in many languages. 
 especially at the beginning or end of a word. 
 To make the English R, pull the tongue back toward the roof of your mouth and don't 
let the tip of your tongue touch your teeth. 
 Practice words like red, car, and better. 
 The L and R distinction for many learners, especially speakers of languages like

 For the L sound, put the tip of your tongue on the roof of your mouth behind your 
upper teeth. 
 For the R sound, pull the tongue back without touching the roof of your mouth. 
 Practice with words like rake.

 Step four, use tongue twisters. 
 Tongue twisters are an excellent way to improve your pronunciation. 
 They help train your mouth to pronounce difficult combinations of sounds more clearly. 
 The more you practice tongue twisters, the more you will develop control over your 
pronunciation. 
 Here are a few examples. 
 She sells seashells by the seashore. 
 Peter Piper picked a pe of pickled peppers. 
 How much wood would a woodchuck chuck if a woodchuck could chuck wood? Start 
by saying them slowly and carefully and then gradually increase your speed as you

 You'll notice that your mouth and tongue become more flexible and and can handle

 Step five, practice stress and intonation. 
 English is a stress timed language, which means that certain syllables are stressed more

 The rhythm of the language depends on the stress pattern, and this affects the way you 
pronounce words. 
 Here's how you can practice. 
 ractice stress and intonation. 
 Listen to the word carefully and repeat it several times stressing the correct syllable. 
 You can also try clapping your hands on the stressed syllable to emphasize the rhythm. 
 Sentence stress and intonation in sentences. 
 Some words are stressed more than others. 
 Usually the important content words, nouns, verbs, adject ives etc. 
 are stressed and the unstressed words, articles, prepositions, auxiliary verbs are said

### 페이지 103

 Building a strong vocabulary is essential if you want to express yourself more clearly, 
speak confidently, and truly master the English language. 
 I know many of you feel that learning vocabulary can be overwhelming. 
 There are so many words and sometimes it feels like no matter how many new words 
you learn, you still forget them or can't use them in the right context. 
 But don't worry, I'm here to tell you that improving your vocabulary does not have to 
be difficult. 
 In fact, with the right strategies, you can learn new words quickly and start using them 
naturally in your speaking and writing. 
 So, let's explore some powerful techniques to help you expand your vocabulary in the 
most effective and enjoyable way possible. 
 Step one, read every day. 
 The best way to learn new words, the first and most natural way to learn new 
vocabulary is through reading. 
 Reading exposes you to new two words in context, helping you understand how they 
are used in real life situations. 
 But let me tell you this, don't just read to understand the meaning of words. 
 Read actively. 
 Pay attention to unfamiliar words and make sure you understand them. 
 When you come across a new word, don't just skip it. 
 Here's how you can make the most out of reading. 
 Read a variety of materials, books, articles, blogs, newspapers, even social media posts. 
 They can all help you expand your vocabulary. 
 Pick materials that interest you so that reading doesn't feel like a chore. 
 Highlight or underline unfamiliar words. 
 If you're reading on paper, highlight the words you don't know.

### 페이지 104

 If you're reading digitally, you Use the built-in features to underline them. 
 Look up the meaning of the word. 
 Use a dictionary to check the meaning, but also check how it's used in context. 
 Many online dictionaries even provide examples of the word in sentences, which is 
really helpful. 
 Practice writing the new word down. 
 Write a sentence using the word you've learned to reinforce it in your memory. 
 For example, If you come across the word serendipity, which means a happy accident or 
finding something good without looking for it, write a sentence like this. 
 I found my favorite book by pure serendipity while cleaning the attic. 
 By reading regularly, you'll not only learn new words, but also improve your 
understanding of how words are used in different contexts. 
 Step two, Two, use flashc cards to reinforce your vocabulary. 
 One of the oldest but most effective methods to learn vocabulary is using flashc cards. 
 Flash cards help reinforce words and their meanings by engaging your memory in an 
active way. 
 Here's how to use flash cards effectively. 
 Write the word on one side of the card and its meaning or a sentence on the other 
side. 
 For example, the word serendipity meaning a happy accident, finding something good 
by chance and a sentence. 
 It was pure serendipity that I found the perfect gift for my friend in that small shop. 
 Review your flash cards regularly. 
 Repetition is key to memorizing new vocabulary. 
 Review your cards at least once a day. 
 Use apps like Ank key quizlet or even traditional paper flashcards to help you organize

### 페이지 105

your vocabulary learning. 
 Use images. 
 You can even add pictures or symbols to help you remember the meaning of a word 
more easily. 
 For example, for serendipity, you could add a picture of someone finding a treasure 
unexpectedly. 
 By consistently using flashcards, you're reinforcing the words in your long-term memory 
and making sure they stick. 
 Step three, learn words in groups or themes. 
 Another effective strategy is to learn words in groups or related themes instead of 
learning isolated words. 
 Grouping them together based on their meaning or context will make it easier to 
remember them and use them correctly. 
 For example, Emotions: Happy, sad, angry, frustrated, excited, anxious, joyful. 
 Travel vocabulary: flight, passport, suitcase, destination, luggage, hotel, tour guide. 
 Business vocabulary: meeting, presentation, deadline, negotiation, client, contract, 
proposal. 
 By Learning words in context or related to specific topics, you will be able to recall 
them more easily and use them in the right situations. 
 Here's how you can start. 
 Pick a theme each week, such as travel, food, or business, and focus on learning  to  
words related to that theme. 
 Create many lists of words you want to learn each week. 
 For example, you could make a list of verbs, run, jump, swim, etc. 
 Adjectives, beautiful, strong, tall, and nouns, dog, house, computer, etc. 
 This method will help you build your vocabulary in a practical way and give you the 
confidence to speak on a variety of topics.

 Step four, use new new words in sentences. 
 Once you learn a new word, don't just memorize it, use it. 
 The best way to make a word stick is by incorporating it into your speaking and writing. 
 Try using new words in sentences, paragraphs, and conversations. 
 Here's how to do this. 
 Write sentences with new words. 
 Practice using them in different contexts. 
 For example, if you learn the word magnificent, you can write, 
 The view from the mountaintop was magnificent. 
 Or, 
 She wore a magnificent red dress to the party. 
 Speak with new words. 
 Don't be afraid to try using your new words when you speak. 
 Start with simple sentences and gradually increase the complexity. 
 The more you practice, the more natural it will become. 
 Ask someone to correct you. 
 If you're practicing with a friend or a teacher, ask them to correct your use of new 
words. 
 This will help you use them properly in future conversations. 
 Using the words you learn in real life contexts will make them feel more natural and

 Learning to speak English is a journey and like any journey, it has its ups and downs.

 The key to success is staying motivated even when things get tough. 
 In this chapter, we'll explore how to stay positive, motivated, and committed to your 
goal of becoming a confident English speaker. 
 Remember, learning a language isn't something that happens overnight. 
 It's a gradual process. 
 As Confucious once said, it does not matter how slowly you go. 
 As long as you do not stop. 
 Let's talk about how you can keep moving forward on this journey no matter the 
challenges you face. 
 One, set small achievable goals. 
 One of the best ways to stay motivated is by setting small achievable goals. 
 These goals should be realistic and measurable so you can easily track your progress. 
 For example, instead of saying, 
 I want to speak English fluently, break that big goal into smaller steps like, 
 I will practice speaking for  minutes a day, or, 
 I will learn five new words this week. 
 When you achieve these small goals, it gives you a sense of accomplishment and keeps 
you motivated to continue. 
 Plus, every small success brings you one step closer to your bigger goal of fluency. 
 Two, track your progress. 
 It's easy to feel stuck or frustrated if you don't see how much progress you're making. 
 That's why it's important to track your improvement.

 Chapter , the power of speaking every day. 
 We've reached the final chapter of our journey and I want to leave you with one simple 
but powerful message. 
 Speak every day. 
 It might sound easy, but this one habit has the potential to transform your English-
speaking skills and boost your confidence from silence to speech. 
 Why is speaking every day so important? Because it builds consistency, creates a 
routine, and helps you get comfortable with the language. 
 Just like learning to ride a like or play a musical instrument. 
 Speaking English takes regular practice. 
 The more you speak, the better you get. 
 Let's explore how this daily habit can truly make a difference and help you achieve your 
goal of speaking English fluently. 
 One, speaking daily builds confidence. 
 When you speak every day, even for just a few minutes, you slowly build your 
confidence. 
 At first, it might feel a little awkward or challenging, but the more you do it, the more 
natural it becomes. 
 Speaking English daily makes it part of your routine, and soon it won't feel like such a 
big task.

### 페이지 136

 Remember, the only way to speak well is to speak often. 
 Two, practice doesn't have to be long. 
 You don't need to spend hours speaking English every day to see progress. 
 Even just  to  minutes of focused speaking practice can make a big difference. 
 You can talk about what you did that day, describe the weather, or even explain a 
recipe. 
 The important thing is to make speaking a regular habit. 
 For example, you can start your day by saying a few sentences in English like, 
 Today I am going to work or I'm planning to read a book. 
 At the end of the Today you can reflect on what you did. 
 I learned some new English words or I went for a walk in the park. 
 These small daily efforts add up and help you become more fluent over time. 
 Three, make speaking a fun part of your day.

 Make speaking English something you look forward to by finding fun and creative ways 
to practice. 
 You can talk about things you enjoy, like your favorite movies, songs, or hobbies. 
 You can even act out simple conversations you might have in real life, like ordering 
food at a restaurant, or asking for directions. 
 The more enjoyable your practice is, the more likely you'll be to stick with it.

 You try to copy the speaker's tone, their speed, their pauses.

 You talk at the same time like a shadow.

하기 위한 어휘력 증진 전략, 실제 의사소통 상황에 대비하기 위한 듣기 능력 향상 팁, 그
리고 독해 및 문법 학습의 효과적인 접근 방식 등이 포함됩니다. 
 또한, 목표 설정, 꾸준한 습관 형성, 그리고 실수에 대한 두려움을 극복하는 등 학습자의 
마음가짐에 대한 조언도 제시됩니다.

영어 번역 습관 극복과 유창성 향상제공된 자료들은 영어 학습자가 흔히 겪는 '번역 습
관'의 한계와 이를 극복하는 것의 중요성을 여러 차례 강조하고 있습니다. 
 번역 습관을 없애는 것은 영어 유창성(fluency) 확보에 있어 매우 중요하며, 이를 위해 
다양한 방법들이 제시됩니다. 
 번역 습관의 문제점 가장 큰 문제는 머릿속으로 모국어 → 영어 변환 과정을 거치면서 
발생하는 망설임과 느려짐입니다 [-]. 
 이 과정은 영어를 유창하게 말하는 데 방해가 되며, 마치 갇혀 있는 듯한 느낌을 줍니
다 [, ]. 
 아는 단어와 문법이 있어도 제때 말이 나오지 않게 되죠 [-]. 
 모국어로 먼저 생각한 뒤 이를 영어로 옮기려 하는 것은 느리고 어색한 과정이며 [, ], 
언어의 자연스러운 흐름을 막습니다 []. 
 번역 습관이 생기는 이유 이러한 습관이 생기는 주요 원인 중 하나는 실수나 틀리는 것
에 대한 두려움입니다 [-]. 
 완벽하게 말하려는 욕심(자존심) 때문에 말을 꺼내기 전에 완벽한 문장을 만들려 하고, 
이 과정에서 번역에 의존하게 됩니다 [, , ]. 
 또한, 듣기나 읽기와 같은 입력(input) 연습에만 치중하고 말하기나 쓰기 같은 출력
(output) 연습이 부족할 때도 번역 습관이 심화될 수 있습니다. 
 듣고 이해하는 것과 직접 말하는 것은 다른 기술이기 때문입니다 [, , ]. 
 입력에만 의존하며 실수를 두려워하면 '출력 근육'이 약해져 막히는 경험을 하게 됩니다 
[]. 
 번역 습관을 없애야 하는 이유 번역 습관을 없애는 것은 자연스럽고 유창한 영어 구사
를 위해 필수적입니다 [, , , ]. 
 머릿속에서 바로 영어로 생각하고 말할 수 있게 되면 망설임이 줄고 자신감이 향상됩니
다 [, , , , -, , , , ]. 
 영어가 단순히 학습 대상이 아닌, 자신을 표현하는 도구이자 제의 집처럼 편안해집니다

 말하기 연습 시작: 가장 중요한 단계입니다 [-, , , ]. 
 완벽해질 때까지 기다리지 말고 지금 바로 말하기를 시작해야 합니다 [, , , , , ].

 말하기는 아는 것을 활용하는 '실행'이며, 이를 통해 영어가 입에 붙게 됩니다 [, ]. 
 혼잣말 연습 (Self-talk): 가장 쉽고 부담 없는 방법 중 하나입니다 [, , , , , , -]. 
 하루 일과나 주변 사물, 생각 등을 영어로 묘사해 보세요 [, , , , , , , ].

 쉐도잉 (Shadowing): 원어민의 말이나 오디오를 듣고 거의 동시에 따라 말하는 연습입
니다 [, , -]. 
 발음, 억양, 리듬을 모방하며 자연스러운 흐름을 익히는 데 도움이 됩니다 [-, ]. 
 번역을 거치지 않고 바로 말을 내뱉는 훈련이 됩니다 []. 
 다른 사람과 대화: 실제 상황에서 영어를 사용하는 가장 효과적인 방법입니다 [, , , , -]. 
 친구, 언어 교환 파트너, 튜터 등과 정기적으로 대화하세요 [, , , , , , ]. 
 처음에는 간단한 주제로 시작하는 것이 좋습니다 []. 
 부담스럽다면 같은 학습자들과 함께 연습할 수도 있습니다 []. 
 실수를 두려워하지 않기: 실수는 학습 과정의 자연스러운 일부이자 개선할 기회입니다

네, 이전 대화에 이어 유튜브 듣기 자료의 텍스트를 기초(Basic), 중급(Intermediate), 그

•  
중급 그래머 인 유즈 (Intermediate Grammar in Use) 관련: 소스 번호부터까지 해당됩
니다. 
•  
기초 그래머 인 유즈 (Grammar in Use Basic) 관련: 소스 번호부터까지 해당됩니다. 
전체 자료에서 추출한 고유한 문장들을 분석하여, 한쪽에만 나오거나 양쪽 모두에 나오
는 문장들을 구분하여 정리했습니다. 
각 문장 뒤에는 해당 문장이 출현한 소스 번호가 모두 표시됩니다.

### 페이지 518

페이지 518 / 524 
 
네, 계속해서 작업을 진행하겠습니다. 
제공해주신 자료들은 다양한 영어 문법 내용을 담고 있습니다. 
이 자료들에 나타난 주요 문법 사항들을 정리하여 말씀드리겠습니다. 
자료에 따르면, 다음과 같은 다양한 영어 문법 개념들이 예문과 함께 설명되고 있습니다. 
•  
현재진행형 (Present Continuous): 지금 말하고 있는 시점에 일어나고 있는 일을 묘사할 
때 사용됩니다. 
예를 들어, 
 Sarah is in her car she is on her way to work she's driving to work, 
 please don't make so much noise i'm trying to work, 
 what are you doing. 
이 시제는 일시적인 상황을 나타낼 때도 사용될 수 있습니다. 
•  
현재시제 (Present Simple): 반복되는 일이나 습관, 일반적인 사실, 영구적인 상황 등을 
나타낼 때 사용됩니다. 
예를 들어, 
 alex is a bus driver but he drives a bus, 
 nurses look after patients in hospitals, 
 i usually go away at weekends, 
 the earth goes round the sun, 
 water boils at ° C. 
제안하거나 약속할 때도 사용됩니다 
i promise I won't be late, 
 What do you suggest I do. 
•

### 페이지 519

페이지 519 / 524 
 
현재완료 (Present Perfect Simple): 과거에 시작된 일이 현재까지 영향을 미치거나, 현재
와 관련된 과거의 경험, 완료된 행동의 결과 등을 나타낼 때 사용됩니다. 
예를 들어, 
 tom can't find his key he's lost his key (결과: 지금 키가 없다), 
 i've cut my finger (결과: 지금 손가락을 다쳤다), 
 have you ever been to China (과거의 경험). 
'just', 'already', 'yet'과 함께 사용되기도 합니다. 
•  
현재완료진행형 (Present Perfect Continuous): 과거에 시작된 행동이 현재까지 계속되고 
있거나, 현재까지 이어져 온 행동의 결과로 현재 상태를 설명할 때 사용됩니다. 
예를 들어, 
 it began raining  hours ago and it is still raining how long has it been raining it's been 
raining for  hours, 
 why are you out of breath have you been running (현재 상태의 원인). 
특정 시점부터 현재까지의 기간을 나타낼 때 'for'나 'since'와 함께 자주 사용됩니다. 
•  
과거시제 (Past Simple): 과거에 시작되어 과거에 끝난 행동이나 상황을 이야기할 때 사
용됩니다. 
예를 들어, 
 Wolf Gang Amadius Mozart was an Austrian musician and composer he lived from  to  
he started composing at the age of  and wrote more than  pieces of music he was only  
years old when he died, 
 i worked in a travel agency now before that I worked in a department store, 
 the police stopped me on my way home last night. 
구체적인 과거 시점(yesterday, last night, a few days ago 등)과 함께 사용됩니다. 
•

### 페이지 520

페이지 520 / 524 
 
과거진행형 (Past Continuous): 과거의 특정 시점에 진행 중이었던 행동을 나타낼 때 사
용됩니다. 
예를 들어, 
 yesterday karen and Joe played tennis they started at : and finished at : so at : they 
were playing tennis, 
 this time last year I was living in Hong Kong, 
 what were you doing at : last night. 
과거진행형은 과거의 한 시점 행동과 다른 과거 행동이 동시에 일어났음을 나타낼 때 
'while'이나 'when'과 함께 사용되기도 합니다 
we were walking home when I met Dan, 
 kate was watching TV when we arrived. 
•  Used to: 과거에는 했지만 지금은 더 이상 하지 않는 습관이나 과거의 상태를 나타낼 
때 사용됩니다. 
예를 들어, 
 i used to play tennis a lot but I don't play very much now, 
 this building is now a furniture shop it used to be a cinema, 
 i used to think Mark was unfriendly but now I realize he's a very nice person. 
부정형은 'didn't use to'로 사용됩니다 
i didn't used to like him. 
 
• 미래 시제: 
현재진행형 (Present Continuous): 이미 계획되거나 정해진 가까운 미래의 일정을 나타
낼 때 사용됩니다. 
예를 들어, Ben의 다음 주 일정을 이야기할 때 
he is playing tennis on Monday afternoon,

### 페이지 521

페이지 521 / 524 
 
 he is meeting Kate on Friday. 
 
be going to: 미래의 계획이나 의도, 또는 현재의 증거에 기반한 미래 예측을 나타낼 때 
사용됩니다. 
예를 들어, 
 i'm tired i'm going to bed now (의도), 
 she's going to buy a new car (계획), 
 look at those black clouds it's going to rain (증거 기반 예측). 
 
will/shall: 미래의 사실, 예측, 즉흥적인 결정, 약속, 제안 등을 나타낼 때 사용됩니다. 
예를 들어, 
 what would you like to drink i'll have orange juice please (즉흥적인 결정), 
 i'll pay you back on Friday (약속), 
 do you think Kate will pass yes she's a good driver she'll pass easily (예측), 
 shall I open the window (제안). 
•  
조동사 (Modals): 능력, 가능성, 의무, 허락, 추측 등을 나타내는 데 사용됩니다. 
 
can: 능력, 가능성, 허락을 나타냅니다 
i can come and see you tomorrow, 
 the word dream can be a noun or a verb, 
 can you speak any foreign languages. 
 
could: 과거의 능력, 과거의 가능성, 제안, 요청 등을 나타냅니다

### 페이지 522

페이지 522 / 524 
 
my grandfather could speak five languages, 
 we could go to the cinema, 
 could you tell me how to get to the bus station. 
또한 비난을 나타낼 때 'could have + 과거분사' 형태로 사용됩니다 
why did you stay at a hotel you could have stayed with me. 
 
may/might: 가능성이나 불확실성을 나타냅니다. 
'may'는 좀 더 공식적인 허락을 나타낼 때도 사용됩니다 
may I sit here. 
'may'와 'might'는 과거의 불확실한 상황을 나타낼 때 'may/might have + 과거분사' 형태
로 사용됩니다 
she may have been asleep, 
 you might have left it at work. 
 
must: 강한 의무나 확실한 추측을 나타냅니다. 
예를 들어, 
 i must go i have to go, 
 you've been traveling all day you must be tired. 
 
have to: 외부의 요건이나 규칙에 의한 의무를 나타냅니다. 
예를 들어, 
 i have to wear glasses for reading, 
 robert can't come out with us this evening he has to work late. 
과거형은 'had to', 부정형은 'don't have to'입니다.

### 페이지 523

페이지 523 / 524 
 
 
should/ought to: 조언, 충고, 기대되는 상황 등을 나타냅니다. 
. 
. 
. 
예를 들어, 
 you look tired you should go to bed, 
 should we invite Stephanie to the party, 
 helen has been studying hard for the exam so she should pass. 
•  
수동태 (Passive Voice): 행위자보다 행동 자체나 행동을 받는 대상에 초점을 맞출 때 사
용됩니다. 
형태는 'be + 과거분사'입니다. 
예를 들어, 
 this house was built in , 
  people are employed by the company, 
 a lot of money was stolen in the robbery. 
조동사나 다양한 시제와 함께 사용될 수 있습니다 
this room will be cleaned later, 
 it might have been sent to the wrong address. 
진행형 수동태는 'be + being + 과거분사' 형태입니다 
the room is being cleaned at the moment. 
자료는 이러한 문법 사항들을 반복적인 예시와 대화를 통해 학습할 수 있도록 구성되어 
있습니다. 
각 문법 단위는 여러 예문을 제시하며 패턴을 익히도록 돕습니다.
